# Skills · R · part 15

721 entries, rbp-binding-site-disruption-analysis to rc-fleet-telematics-fuel-level-smoothing. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **rbp-binding-site-disruption-analysis**: Use when you have a catalog of splicing QTLs (e.g. from sQTLseekeR2 on GTEx) plus ENCODE eCLIP peak sets and want to identify which RBPs' binding sites are altered by sQTLs relative to non-sQTL variants. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rbp-binding-site-disruption-analysis))
- **rbp-binding-target-threshold-filtering**: Use when you have a MAPIT-seq differential editing statistics table (e.g., RBP_Edit_GE_RPE_DATA.tsv from the calltargets step) and need to define the RBP's target gene set under default thresholds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/rbp-binding-target-threshold-filtering))
- **rbp-candidate-validation-against-gold-standard**: Use when an NMF (or other) clustering of candidate RBPs has produced cluster assignments and you must test whether any single cluster is significantly enriched for known gold-standard circRNA regulators before accepting the clustering strategy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rbp-candidate-validation-against-gold-standard))
- **rbp-cluster-comparison-statistics**: Use when a merged CRIT feature matrix plus NMF cluster membership labels are available and the goal is to rank features (e.g., Zg splicing similarity, Zp alteration proportion) that separate predicted circRNA regulators from other RBPs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rbp-cluster-comparison-statistics))
- **rbp-ncrna-interaction-network-construction**: Use when building the prior interaction graph needed to search RBP-ncRNA circuits (RNCs) from multi-omics differential expression scores, e.g. TCGA ESCA/BRCA or APP/PS1 mouse data scored with fRNC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rbp-ncrna-interaction-network-construction))
- **rbp-ncrna-network-module-scoring**: Use when detected RNC modules (global or local, e.g. from runmodule with FDR = 1e-5) need permutation-based validation of whether their summary scores exceed what random node sets of the same size would achieve. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/rbp-ncrna-network-module-scoring))
- **rbp-target-identification**: Use when calling RBP target transcripts from per-sample MAPIT-seq RNA editing event tables (e.g. *_Edit_*_DATA.tsv), comparing a deaminase-expressing treatment against its control to recover bound RNAs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/rbp-target-identification))
- **rbp-transcript-region-distribution-analysis**: Use when eCLIP replicates (e.g., WT vs point-mutant RBP) have Skipper reproducible_enriched_windows output and the goal is to compare the fraction of binding windows assigned to transcript regions such as CDS, deep introns, or tRNA loci. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/rbp-transcript-region-distribution-analysis))
- **rbpex-seeding-investigation**: Investigate slow or failing RBPEX seeding in SQL Azure VLDB configurations. Covers root cause analysis across noisy neighbors, IO contention, redo thread behavior, node-level resource saturation, and INUPSLO timing. Uses Kusto queries against MonSocrates, MonRgLoad, MonSqlRgHistory, MonAnalyticsDBSnapshot, and MonManagement tables. ([sqlcss/copilot-agents](https://github.com/sqlcss/copilot-agents/tree/HEAD/.github/agents/skills/kql-templates/sqldb/socrates/rbpex-seeding-investigation) · ★1)

Use when the user says "test two versions", "A/B test", "experiment with the price/tutorial/offer", or when a past test flip-flopped, "won" then regressed after shipping, or nobody can say which arm a player saw. Use for a one-off analysis of pasted dashboard data: how a game is doing, what to change next. The recurring per-game weekly loop is rbx-weekly. - **rbx-analytics-events**: AnalyticsService instrumentation that survives launch — one wrapper module, a disciplined event taxonomy, economy/funnel/progression/custom events logged server-side within rate limits. Use when the user says "add analytics", "track events", "instrument the economy", "log purchases/kills/levels", or when the dashboard is empty, numbers look impossibly high, or events silently stopped appearing. Do not use for interpreting the resulting charts — load rbx-analytics-reading — or for multi-step drop-off analysis — load rbx-analytics-funnels instead. ([blushneybusiness-glitch/roblox-omniskills](https://github.com/blushneybusiness-glitch/roblox-omniskills/tree/HEAD/skills/rbx-analytics-events))
- **rbx-analytics-funnels**: Funnels that answer one question each — designed backwards from a decision, instrumented in order, and read with a drop-off diagnosis procedure that names the fix. Use when the user says "where do players drop off", "onboarding funnel", "shop conversion", "why does nobody finish the tutorial", or when a funnel chart shows steps out of order, flat tails, or a single-step cliff. Do not use for the raw event plumbing — load rbx-analytics-events — or for judging whether the resulting rates are healthy — load rbx-analytics-reading instead.
